- **task-graph-basics**: Foundation skill for task-graph-mcp - connection workflow, tool reference, task trees, search, and shared patterns for multi-worker coordination ([Oortonaut/task-graph-mcp](https://github.com/Oortonaut/task-graph-mcp/tree/HEAD/config/skills/task-graph-basics))
- **task-graph-builder**: Build a deterministic task graph from a playbook and objective. Use for task graphs. Do NOT use for unapproved remote writes. ([paulosantanna/ai-assistent-universal-coder](https://github.com/paulosantanna/ai-assistent-universal-coder/tree/HEAD/skills/task-graph-builder) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **task-graph-classifier**: Decompose a mathematical modeling problem into a subproblem task graph, identify task tags for each subproblem, analyze dependencies, and recommend follow-up modeling skills. ([YYUmath/MathModelingWorkflow](https://github.com/YYUmath/MathModelingWorkflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/task-graph-classifier) · ★3)
- **task-graph-commit-lock**: Git commit serialization pattern using task-graph claim/release as a mutex - prevents concurrent git operations in multi-agent workflows ([Oortonaut/task-graph-mcp](https://github.com/Oortonaut/task-graph-mcp/tree/HEAD/task-graph/skills/task-graph-commit-lock))
- **task-graph-contextual-integrity-gating**: Prevent multi-hop privacy leakage and compositional mosaic disclosure in multi-agent workflows using Task-Graph Contextual Integrity (TG-CI), maximal admissible propagation subtrees (Auto-Region), and zero-LLM semantic frontier gates. Derived from Wu et al. (arXiv:2610.39532). ([Marontis/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/Marontis/agentic-engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/task-graph-contextual-integrity-gating) · ★3)
- **task-graph-executor**: Decompose complex user tasks into multi-step workflows using DAG-based execution. ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/agent-workflow-skill) · ★10)
- **task-graph-generation**: Local 任务拆解 Plugin skill for AI 驱动的任务拆解插件：将需求或设计拆解为实现计划、任务图、里程碑和执行策略。 ([yhangf/csc-plugins](https://github.com/yhangf/csc-plugins/tree/HEAD/cospowers-task-planning-plugin/skills/task-graph-generation))
- **task-graph-manager**: Build and maintain the task graph for Autoflow. - **task-graphs**: Design agent orchestration as an explicit task graph before spawning any subagent. Use when planning multi-agent work, writing a Workflow script, fanning out subagents, reviewing an existing pipeline, or deciding whether work should be parallel or sequential. Covers the shape test (does the work split?), fake-edge deletion, the diamond pattern (parallel workers, separate verifier contexts, one owned merge), the stop rule, human gates on irreversible edges, and the four guardrail caps. Applies directly to Claude Code subagents and Workflow scripts. ([rjmoggach/claude-agent-engineering-plugin](https://github.com/rjmoggach/claude-agent-engineering-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/task-graphs) · ★1)
